# PRD-101 — Vendor the six public documents and write the profile

## In plain words

**The problem today.** The open-banking product is a long design document and nothing else. It plans an old version of the standard, and no file of the product holds the standard itself. Later work on the product has no fixed, checked description of the interface it must serve.

**What changes.** The product gains the publisher's six interface documents of the current standard, copied byte for byte, and a profile file that names them, counts their operations and states the security rules and status codes. A small Go package loads and checks the profile, its tests run on every pull request, and the old design document is replaced by a short description.

**Done means:**

- The six interface documents of the standard are in the product folder, and each one has the checksum of the publisher's file.
- A test fails when a document or the profile is changed: it checks every checksum, the operation counts 29, 41, 9, 4, 5 and 1, the totals of 63 served, 1 called and 25 not offered, and the signature-header counts 15, 5 and 2.
- Every consent status code in the profile is one that the documents declare.
- The source of the documents and the rule of the standard that an operation not offered answers 404 are written down beside them.
- The tests of the product run on every pull request that changes it.
- The product's description is a short page that names what is built and the decision record.

The rest of this ticket is the exact instruction for the model that does the work.

RULES OF CONDUCT:

1. Do one work package at a time. Its ticket is the whole specification.
2. Before you change anything, run the precondition and every pre-flight command. If any output differs from the ticket, stop this package and file the wrong report.
3. Change only files under "Allowed paths". If the work needs any other file, stop this package and file the wrong report.
4. Never edit a chart `version:`, an `appVersion:`, a slot pin, a catalogue pin or an image tag line. CI writes them. If your ticket changes a `blueprint.yaml` or a slot file, run `node scripts/build-catalog.mjs` in `products/catalyst/bootstrap/ui` and commit the two files it writes; do not edit them by hand.
5. Never edit `docs/ledger/plan.yaml`, `docs/ledger/stability.yaml` or any file under `docs/adr/`, unless the ticket lists that exact file. In `docs/ledger/gates.yaml` change only the lines the ticket quotes.
6. Make no design decision. If the ticket does not say which of two things to do, stop this package and file the wrong report.
7. Do not rename, move, reformat or "clean up" anything the ticket does not name.
8. Do not delete or weaken a test, a guard or a check to make it pass. Do not add `|| true`, `continue-on-error`, a skip, or a wider allow-list unless the ticket names that exact line.
9. Do not add a feature flag that is off by default. Do not add a fallback value for data the ticket says exists. Do not add a field, a flag or a setting that nothing in your own change reads.
10. Evidence is a CI check or a gate record. Your own terminal output is not evidence. Never paste an output you did not get from the command.
11. A command that prints a result is not a pass. Read the exit code. Zero is pass; anything else is fail.
12. A check that is pending is not green. Never report a pull request as ready while a check has not finished. You never merge a pull request: the architect reviews and merges every one.
13. Never run a command against a live cluster or a cloud account unless the ticket's lane is GATE and the command is written in the ticket.
14. Never create a NodePort, in a chart or on a cluster, for any reason.
15. Never use `git push --force`, `git reset --hard` on a shared branch, `git add -A`, or `git commit --amend` on a pushed commit.
16. Commit with the identity given in the ticket. Write `Refs #N`, never `Closes #N`. End the pull-request body with `Work-Package: <ID>`. A wrong report is the one exception: its commit and its pull request carry no `Refs` line.
17. Put no secret, token, key or password in a commit, a pull request, an issue or a log.
18. Use the terms in `docs/GLOSSARY.md`. Do not use a banned term. In code, commits and documents cite only work-package identifiers, decision-record numbers and the tracking issue as `Refs #N`.
19. If a step fails, do that step again once. If it fails again, stop this package and file the wrong report. Do not try a different approach.
20. One session does one package: the one the architect named. When it is reported or stopped, give your report as your final message and stop. Never take another package by yourself, and do not ask anyone whether to continue.
21. Report only what you did and which checks passed. Do not write "should work", "expected to pass" or "verified" without naming the check run.
22. If you find a defect that is not in your ticket, do not fix it. Add one line to `docs/ledger/plan/found/<ID>.md` with the file, the line and what you saw.
23. Never open, search or quote any repository other than the platform repository.
24. Never wait with `sleep`. The finish step "checks" has the one command that waits. While your pull request waits for its checks, do no other work.
25. Do not open a pull request with the trailer `Work-Package: PLAN`. Only the architect does.

## 1. Supersede banner
This ticket is the complete specification. It supersedes every earlier comment, issue text and conversation. Any deviation fails the ticket.

## 2. End goal
`products/fingate` holds the first standards profile of the open-banking product, `profiles/rw-4.0.0/`. It holds the six OpenAPI documents of the Read/Write API 4.0.0, exactly as the publisher released them, `profile.yaml` naming them with their SHA-256 and their operation counts, and `SOURCE.md` recording where they came from and the clause of the standard that sets the answer 404 for an operation that is not offered. The Go module `github.com/openova-io/openova/products/fingate` has one package, `internal/profile`, that loads and checks a profile; its tests prove every checksum and every count. The module's tests run on every pull request that changes it, through the path filters and the `go-modules` matrix of `.github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml`. The old 416-line `products/fingate/README.md` is replaced in place by a page of 22 lines.

## 3. The model (the law)
1. The six files `products/fingate/profiles/rw-4.0.0/*-openapi.yaml` are byte for byte the files of folder `dist/openapi/` of the publisher's repository `OpenBankingUK/read-write-api-specs` at tag `v4.0-Update-5`, commit `5af5621e1b68dda5a0d821f2ffee9d282cfe550a`. Their SHA-256 values are those of step 6. They are never edited. This ticket carries them as one archive block; the executor never downloads them.
2. `vrp-openapi.yaml` has Windows line endings in the publisher's file. `products/fingate/profiles/rw-4.0.0/.gitattributes` turns off line-ending conversion for the six documents, so the bytes stay the same in every checkout.
3. `profile.yaml` is the block `profile.yaml` of this ticket. Its fields: `schema: openova.io/fingate-profile/v1`; `id: rw-4.0.0` (the folder name); `basePath: /open-banking/v4.0`; `notImplementedStatus: 404`; `documents`, one entry per document with `api`, `file`, `sha256`, `operations`, `served` and, for the one document the product calls, `called`; `notOffered`, eight path prefixes of the documents' paths (the international and file payments); `security`; `consentStatus`, the wire code per internal consent state and per consent family; `idempotencyHours: 24`.
4. The package `internal/profile` (block `profile.go`) rejects a profile with an unknown or a missing field, and checks: the schema; the id equals the folder name; `basePath` starts with `/`; `notImplementedStatus` is from 400 to 499; each document has a unique `api` and a unique plain file name, a 64-hex `sha256`, and counts that fit; each `notOffered` prefix starts with `/`; `security.fapi` is `fapi1-advanced-final`; `security.clientAuth` holds only `tls_client_auth` and `private_key_jwt`; `security.signingAlgorithms` holds only `PS256`; `security.consentCarrier` is `intent-claim` or `authorization-details`; `security.accessTokenSeconds` is from 60 to 600; `consentStatus` has exactly the families `account-access`, `funds-confirmation`, `payment` and `vrp`, each with codes for `awaiting`, `authorised` and `rejected`, states only from `awaiting`, `authorised`, `rejected`, `revoked`, `expired`, `consumed`, the state `consumed` only for `payment`, and every code four capital letters; `idempotencyHours` is at least 1. For each document it checks the SHA-256 of the file, that it is OpenAPI 3.0, that its operation count equals `operations`, and that `served` plus `called` plus the operations under a `notOffered` prefix equals `operations`. A path lies under a prefix when it equals the prefix or starts with the prefix followed by `/`. An operation is signed when it declares the header `x-jws-signature` as required.
5. The tests (block `profile_test.go`) assert each SHA-256, the operation counts 29, 41, 9, 4, 5 and 1, the totals of 89 operations, 63 served, 1 called and 25 not offered, the signed operations 15 (payment initiation), 5 (variable recurring payments) and 2 (events) and 0 elsewhere, the exact `consentStatus` map, that each of its codes is in the `Data.Status` enumeration of the family's consent response schema in the documents, that every document is OpenAPI 3.0.0 with `info.version` 4.0.0, and, with an in-memory fixture, that each rule of law 4 can fail: one failing case per rule, among them a second document with the same `api`, a second document with the same file name, counts that do not fit, a document that is not OpenAPI 3.0, a signature header that is not required, and a path that only starts with the letters of a prefix.
6. `SOURCE.md` (block `SOURCE.md`) quotes the clause of the standard: "If an ASPSP has not implemented an API endpoint, it **must** respond with a 404 (Not Found) for requests to that URL." with its address.
7. `products/fingate/README.md` is rewritten in place (block `README.md`). It is not deleted, so nothing is added to `docs/ledger/deleted-paths.txt`.
8. The module is tested on every pull request that changes it: `.github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml` runs on a change under `products/fingate/` (path filters of `pull_request` and `push`), `products/fingate` is one entry of its `go-modules` matrix, and `scripts/check-modules-tested.py` passes. The module's own workflow is a later package. `docs/ledger/untested-modules.txt` does not change; it may only shrink.
9. Nothing else is added: no server, no chart, no Blueprint, no generated code, no other workflow.

## 4. Measured current state
Measured at commit `435fea9078d666e9b0d68cb218a12b9ff205bf8b` on 2026-11-13.

- The folder holds one file. `git ls-files -- products/fingate` prints `products/fingate/README.md`.
- That README has 416 lines and plans an old version of the standard. `wc -l < products/fingate/README.md` prints `416`; `sed -n '148p' products/fingate/README.md` prints `| UK Open Banking | 3.1.10 | Planned |`; `grep -c 'uk-ob-3.1' products/fingate/README.md` prints `1`.
- No `go.mod` exists in the folder; `python3 scripts/check-modules-tested.py` prints `OK: 35 Go modules, each tested on pull requests or listed`. That guard finds the modules with `git ls-files`: it sees a `go.mod` only when git tracks it.
- The `go-modules` matrix of `.github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml` lists 11 modules, from `core/marketplace-api` to `products/sandbox/pty-server`, and not `products/fingate` (`grep -c -F 'products/fingate' .github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml` prints `0`). In the matrix the line `        module:` is directly followed by the comment line that starts `          # core/pool-domain-manager is deliberately NOT in this list`; the two lines together occur once (the command of pre-flight P2 prints `1`). Its path filters name each tested module folder, `'**/go.mod'` and `'.github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml'`, the last one once under `pull_request` and once under `push` (`grep -c -x -F "      - '.github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml'" .github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml` prints `2`). A new `go.mod` that no pull-request workflow tests turns the check `every Go module is tested on pull requests, or listed` red, and the list of untested modules may not grow.
- The publisher's documents, measured by the author of this ticket in the publisher's public repository `https://github.com/OpenBankingUK/read-write-api-specs`: the tag `v4.0-Update-5` is commit `5af5621e1b68dda5a0d821f2ffee9d282cfe550a`, the newest tag whose six documents carry `info.version: 4.0.0` (the tag `v4.0.1` carries the next version). All six are OpenAPI 3.0.0. Operations: account information 29, payment initiation 41 (16 domestic, 17 international, 8 file), variable recurring payments 9, confirmation of funds 4, events 5, event notifications 1. The required header `x-jws-signature` is declared on 15, 5 and 2 operations of payment initiation, variable recurring payments and events, and on none of the others. The consent `Data.Status` enumerations are: account access `AWAU RJCT AUTH EXPD CANC`; domestic payment consents `AWAU RJCT AUTH COND`; funds confirmation `AWAU RJCT AUTH CANC EXPD`; variable recurring payments `AWAU RJCT AUTH CANC EXPD`. Every document names the publisher's open licence in `info.license`.
- The clause on unimplemented endpoints is on the publisher's page `https://openbankinguk.github.io/read-write-api-site3/v4.0/profiles/read-write-data-api-profile.html`, part "400 (Bad Request) v/s 404 (Not Found)", read by the author of this ticket on 2026-11-13.
- (after PRD-020) `docs/adr/0034-later-products-ai-hub-and-open-banking.md` exists. The new README links it. At commit `435fea9078d666e9b0d68cb218a12b9ff205bf8b` it does not exist yet: this package comes after `PRD-020`.

## 9. Execution constraints
- Branch: `wp/PRD-101`, cut from `origin/main`. One branch, one pull request.
- Session: the architect starts one session for one ticket. You do this ticket and no other. After your report you stop.
- Commit identity: every commit is made with `git -c user.name=hatiyildiz -c user.email=269457768+hatiyildiz@users.noreply.github.com commit`. The work is one commit. A further commit is made only to repair a red check or a failure of the fence run, or when the architect's review asks for a change.
- The commit message and the pull-request body cite the tracking issue as `Refs #<issue>`. Never write `Closes` or `Fixes`.
- The pull-request body ends with the line `Work-Package: PRD-101`.
- Order: the architect starts this ticket only when these packages are merged: `FND-021`, `FND-022`, `FND-023`.
- The plan entry: the checks `work-package / scope`, `work-package / precondition` and `work-package / acceptance` judge by this package's entry in `docs/ledger/plan.yaml` on `main`. (They are the jobs `scope`, `precondition` and `acceptance` of the workflow `work-package`; a command that lists checks prints the job name alone.) The architect writes that entry from this ticket: its `touches` are the paths of field 14, its precondition is field 12, its acceptance commands are those of field 18, and its `ticket_rev` is 1. `work-package / precondition` runs the precondition on the merge base, that is the state before your change. `work-package / acceptance` runs the acceptance commands on your change merged into `main`, each with `bash -euo pipefail -c`. `work-package / scope` judges the changed paths, the budget, the order and the marker file.
- Checks: every check that runs on the head commit of the pull request must finish green. `python3 scripts/plan.py checks --pr <pr> --wait` reads them. Exit code 0: all finished green. Exit code 1: one finished red. Exit code 2: one has not finished. Exit code 4: one has not finished for 90 minutes. Exit code 3: the tool could not read the checks. Only code 0 is green.
- Merge: you do not merge. When every check has finished green you report on the pull request. The architect reviews every pull request, merges it and closes the tracking issue. If the architect asks for a change, the architect starts a session with this ticket again, and you make the change inside the allowed paths as a further commit.
- A file that this ticket embeds with a checksum is never edited by hand. If such a file is wrong, do its copy step again. If its checksum still differs, or a check fails on a file that matches its checksum, the ticket is wrong.
- Budget: at most 60 files added or modified and 3,000 changed lines (size M). A deleted file counts as one line and no file. Your own files under `docs/ledger/plan` are not counted.

For this ticket (where a note below differs from a fixed step, follow the note):

- **No download.** The six documents come from the block `documents.tar.gz.b64` of this ticket. Do not open, clone or download the publisher's repository or any other repository. The first `go test` or `go vet` may download the Go module `gopkg.in/yaml.v3` v3.0.1 through the Go module proxy; that is expected.

The fence run and the check `work-package / scope` judge by it, and stop condition 5 means this budget. The change counts 15 files and 32,024 changed lines as the scope fence counts them: 30,786 lines are the six documents and 1,238 lines are the rest.

Step 18 has judged it before, with the guard's own function. The check `work-package / acceptance` judges it on the merged change.

```
products/fingate/profiles/rw-4.0.0/.gitattributes
```

Besides these paths, three files of your own are always allowed: `docs/ledger/plan/done/PRD-101.yaml` (the marker), `docs/ledger/plan/wrong/PRD-101-rev1.yaml` (a wrong report) and `docs/ledger/plan/found/PRD-101.md` (defects you saw and did not fix). No other file may change.

## 15. Forbidden paths
- `docs/ledger/deleted-paths.txt`: the README is rewritten, not deleted.
- `docs/ledger/untested-modules.txt`: the list may only shrink; the module is tested instead.
- `.github/workflows/fingate-ci.yaml`: package PRD-106.
- `products/fingate/internal/gen/**`, `products/fingate/tools/**`: package PRD-102.
- `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/STATUS.md`: not this package.

## 16. Steps
**How to read an exit code.** Every command line runs in a shell of its own, so an exit code is printed by the same line that produced it: a command of this ticket that ends in `; echo "exit code: $?"` prints it as its last line. Never read it with a second command.

**How long a command may run.** Give every command of this ticket a time limit of 10 minutes: the `timeout` of the Bash tool, 600000. Never wait with `sleep`: the session refuses a long `sleep`. The one step that waits is the finish step "checks", and its command waits by itself.

**Start.** Do these first, in order. If the architect's first message says that you are going on with this package after an interruption or after a review: run `git fetch origin --prune`, run `git switch wp/PRD-101`, do the step "identity", and go on with the step that the message names.

- **Identity.** Run `gh api user --jq .login`. It must print `hatiyildiz` or `emrahbaysal`. If it prints another name, or fails, this session has no usable GitHub profile: change nothing, push nothing, give that output as your final message and stop.
- **Branch.** Run `git status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | wc -l`. It must print `0`: the checkout is clean. If it prints another number, change nothing, give the output of `git status --porcelain` as your final message and stop. Run `git fetch origin --prune`. Run `git switch -c wp/PRD-101 origin/main`.
- **Claim.** First see that nobody holds this package: `git ls-remote --exit-code --heads origin wp/PRD-101 > /dev/null; echo "exit code: $?"`. The last line must be `exit code: 2`: the branch is not on the remote. If it is `exit code: 0`, another session holds this package: change nothing, give that output as your final message and stop. Then run `git push -u origin wp/PRD-101`. This push is the claim; it changes no file.
- **Claim check.** Run `python3 scripts/plan.py claim-check PRD-101`. If it prints `keep`: go on. If it prints `yield`: run `git push origin --delete wp/PRD-101`, give `yield` as your final message and stop. If the command fails because `scripts/plan.py` does not exist, the two bootstrap packages are not merged and this ticket must not start: run `git push origin --delete wp/PRD-101`, report that, and stop.
- **Pre-flight.** Run the precondition of field 12. Then run every pre-flight command of field 13. An item that states "Expected output" is compared by what it prints, character for character; its exit code does not matter. An item that states "Expected exit code" is compared by its exit code only. If one differs, do not change anything: follow "If this ticket is wrong" at the end of this ticket.
- **Tracking issue.** The architect opened the tracking issue of this package when this ticket was written. Find it: `gh issue list --search 'PRD-101 in:title' --state open`. Each line it prints holds the number of an issue, its state and its title. The line whose title starts with `PRD-101 ` is the tracking issue, and its number is `<issue>` in the steps below. If two lines have such a title, take the lower number. If no line has such a title, open the issue yourself: `gh api -X POST repos/openova-io/openova/issues -f title='PRD-101 Vendor the six public documents and write the profile' -f body='Ticket: docs/ledger/plan/tickets/PRD-101.md' --jq .number`; the number it prints is `<issue>`. Then mark the issue as started: `printf '{"labels":["status/in-progress"]}' | gh api -X POST repos/openova-io/openova/issues/<issue>/labels --input - --jq '.[].name'`. It prints the labels of the issue, one per line. One of them must be `status/in-progress`.

**Work.** The steps of this package, in order.

Run every command from the repository root. A step that copies a block reads this ticket at the path `docs/ledger/plan/tickets/PRD-101.md`. Where a step says "stop", change nothing more and follow "If this ticket is wrong" at the end of this ticket.

The work on files ends here. Go on with the finish steps. Field 9 has notes on the finish steps "acceptance run" and "red check": read them before you start.

**Finish.** Do these after the last work step, in order.

- **Acceptance run.** Run `python3 scripts/plan.py accept --id PRD-101; echo "exit code: $?"`. It runs every acceptance command of field 18 the way the check `work-package / acceptance` runs them. The last line must be `exit code: 0`. If it is not, the output names the command that failed: correct your work inside the allowed paths and run it again. A file that this ticket embeds with a checksum is not corrected by hand: do its copy step again instead. If a command still fails after one correction, follow "If this ticket is wrong" with stage `acceptance`. This run finds mistakes early. It is not evidence.
- **Marker.** Write the marker file with this one command: `mkdir -p docs/ledger/plan/done && printf 'schema: openova.io/plan-done/v1\nid: PRD-101\nticket_rev: 1\n' > docs/ledger/plan/done/PRD-101.yaml`. The file has three lines, each starting at the first column.
- **Changed paths.** Run `git status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | cut -c4-`. It prints one path per line: every file that is changed or new. Each path must be matched by a line of the code block of field 14, or be `docs/ledger/plan/done/PRD-101.yaml` or `docs/ledger/plan/found/PRD-101.md`. If one path is not, a file outside the allowed paths changed: follow "If this ticket is wrong".
- **Staging.** Stage every path that the step "changed paths" printed, by name: `git add -- <path> <path>`. Never use `git add -A` and never `git add .`.
- **Commit.** Make the one commit of the work: `git -c user.name=hatiyildiz -c user.email=269457768+hatiyildiz@users.noreply.github.com commit -m 'test(fingate): vendor the Read/Write API 4.0.0 documents and load them as a profile (PRD-101)' -m 'Refs #<issue>'`.
- **Body file.** Write the block of field 21 into the file `/tmp/PRD-101-body.md`: the lines between its two fence lines, without the fence lines, with `<issue>` replaced by the number.
- **Fence run.** Run the scope fence yourself before you push: `python3 scripts/plan.py check-pr --base origin/main --head HEAD --body-file /tmp/PRD-101-body.md; echo "exit code: $?"`. This is the judgement of the check `work-package / scope`. The last line must be `exit code: 0`. If it is not, the lines above it name each failure. A path outside field 14, a change over the budget or a package of the order line that is not merged: follow "If this ticket is wrong". A failure about the pull-request body: correct `/tmp/PRD-101-body.md` and run this step again. Anything else that you can correct inside the allowed paths: correct it, stage the corrected paths by name, commit with the same identity and the message `fix: repair work-package / scope (PRD-101)`, and run this step again.
- **Push and pull request.** Run `git push origin wp/PRD-101`. Open the pull request: `gh pr create --base main --head wp/PRD-101 --title 'test(fingate): vendor the Read/Write API 4.0.0 documents and load them as a profile (PRD-101)' --body-file /tmp/PRD-101-body.md`. The command prints the address of the pull request. The number at its end is `<pr>`.
- **Checks.** Run `python3 scripts/plan.py checks --pr <pr> --wait; echo "exit code: $?"`. The command reads the checks of the head commit. While one has not finished it waits and reads again, for about 90 seconds. The number in the last line is the result. While it is 2, run the same command again. If it is 4, a check has not finished for 90 minutes: follow "If this ticket is wrong" with stage `check` and, as ref, the name of a check that the output marks `unfinished`. If it is 3, the tool could not read the checks: run the command once more, and if it is 3 again follow "If this ticket is wrong" with stage `check` and the ref `checks`.
- **Red check.** If the result is 1: `gh pr checks <pr>` lists the checks with a link each. In the link of the red check, the number after `/runs/` is its run and the number after `/job/` is its job. Read why it failed: `gh api repos/openova-io/openova/actions/jobs/<job>/logs | grep -B 40 -m 1 '##\[error\]' | cut -c30-300`. It prints the 40 lines before the first error line of the job. If it prints nothing, read the end of the log: `gh api repos/openova-io/openova/actions/jobs/<job>/logs | tail -40 | cut -c30-300`. To see whether a check is also red on `main`: `gh api "repos/openova-io/openova/commits/$(git rev-parse origin/main)/check-runs?per_page=100" --paginate --jq '.check_runs[] | select(.name=="<name of the check>") | .conclusion'`; if it prints `failure`, the reason is not in your change. Three cases. The reason is not in your change (the check is red on `main` too, or the job failed before it reached your files): change nothing, run `gh run rerun <run>` once, and go back to the step "checks". Your change caused it and the file is one you wrote by hand: correct the mistake inside the allowed paths only, stage the corrected paths by name, make a further commit with the same identity and the message `fix: repair <name of the check> (PRD-101)`, push, and go back to the step "checks". Your change caused it and the file is one this ticket embeds with a checksum: do not edit it; follow "If this ticket is wrong" with stage `check`. In every case, if the same check is red a second time, follow "If this ticket is wrong" with stage `check`. In a wrong report with stage `check`, unless this ticket says otherwise: `ref` is the name of the red check, `command` is the log command above with the job number in it, `expected` is `the check finishes green`, and `actual` is what the log command printed.
- **Merge.** If the result is 0: first do the before-merge steps of field 9, if this ticket has any. Then say on the pull request that it is ready: `gh api -X POST repos/openova-io/openova/issues/<pr>/comments -f body='Ready for review. Every check finished green. Acceptance commands A1 to A4 were run by the check work-package / acceptance.' --jq .html_url`. You do not merge.
- **Report.** Give your report as your final message: the address of the pull request, the last output of the step "checks", and the result of each before-merge step of field 9 if this ticket has any. Then stop. The architect reviews the pull request, merges it, closes the tracking issue and starts the next ticket. Do not take another package, and do not ask whether to continue.

## 17. Stop conditions
1. The precondition of field 12 or a pre-flight output of field 13 differs from the expected value.
2. The work needs a file that field 14 does not allow.
3. A step cannot be done as it is written, or the ticket does not say which of two things to do.
4. An acceptance command fails after you have corrected the mistake once, or the same pull-request check is red twice.
5. The change is larger than the budget of field 9.
6. A checksum of step 3, 6 or 15 still differs after the one repeat that step allows.
7. The command of step 16 prints `not changed`, or step 17 still differs after one repeat.
8. Step 18 does not print `['products/fingate']` and `exit code: 0`.
9. Step 19 does not end with `exit code: 0`.
10. Step 21 prints another path, or a line is missing.

## 18. Acceptance checks
**A1.** The module vets, and every test of the profile package passes: the checksums, the operation, served, called and not-offered counts, the signature-header counts, the consent codes against the documents' enumerations, and each rule of the loader failing on a broken fixture. Without the work there is no module and the command fails.
```sh
(cd products/fingate && go vet ./... && go test -count=1 ./...)
```

**A2.** Every file of the package under `products/fingate` is the file of this ticket: the six publisher documents and the eight embedded files. It fails when a file is missing or changed.

**A3.** Every Go module, the new one included, is tested on pull requests or listed, and the list of untested modules did not grow. Without the matrix entry the new module is untested and the guard fails.
```sh
python3 scripts/check-modules-tested.py
```

**A4.** The pull-request workflow runs on a change under `products/fingate/` and tests the module: `products/fingate/**` is in the path filters of `pull_request` and of `push`, and the `go-modules` matrix lists `products/fingate` exactly once. Without the change each count is zero.
```sh
python3 -c 'import yaml; w = yaml.safe_load(open(".github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml", encoding="utf-8")); m = w["jobs"]["go-modules"]["strategy"]["matrix"]["module"]; on = w[True]; n = [m.count("products/fingate")] + [on[e]["paths"].count("products/fingate/**") for e in ("pull_request", "push")]; print(n); raise SystemExit(0 if n == [1, 1, 1] else 1)'
```

Revert the squash commit. It removes the module, the profile, the documents and the matrix entry together, and restores the old README.

```yaml
# Standards profile rw-4.0.0: the Read/Write API 4.0.0 of the open-banking standard.
# Loaded and checked by products/fingate/internal/profile. Where the documents come from: SOURCE.md.
schema: openova.io/fingate-profile/v1
id: rw-4.0.0
basePath: /open-banking/v4.0
notImplementedStatus: 404
documents:
  - {api: account-info,        file: account-info-openapi.yaml,        sha256: 7d9297935e11971173664ba793c14b6fd64e162a97d2de46967cd7f9cc0ca792, operations: 29, served: 29}
  - {api: payment-initiation,  file: payment-initiation-openapi.yaml,  sha256: 1cb84c8b6972cb56f414448af94f5c7595d6ebe13a5c41deeba5904e04efb41e, operations: 41, served: 16}
  - {api: vrp,                 file: vrp-openapi.yaml,                 sha256: f547f3cbc465970c65a744dce9c28fa5f0201fb5f07b8f35af79942e3c64fb20, operations: 9,  served: 9}
  - {api: confirmation-funds,  file: confirmation-funds-openapi.yaml,  sha256: 2ab1717555e8e960980be39f07821e1c0d280662a0fbb84dd562168cb5604358, operations: 4,  served: 4}
  - {api: events,              file: events-openapi.yaml,              sha256: ba248d53243ddad30ee856e175f8df38669eac2b1015c7c10d88db56afbcb642, operations: 5,  served: 5}
  - {api: event-notifications, file: event-notifications-openapi.yaml, sha256: c60932f376472697c22af602d4deba5842921732aa547ef71d6c41a67389908a, operations: 1,  served: 0, called: 1}
# Prefixes of the documents' paths. An operation under one of them answers notImplementedStatus.
notOffered:
  - /international-payment-consents
  - /international-payments
  - /international-scheduled-payment-consents
  - /international-scheduled-payments
  - /international-standing-order-consents
  - /international-standing-orders
  - /file-payment-consents
  - /file-payments
security:
  fapi: fapi1-advanced-final
  clientAuth: [tls_client_auth, private_key_jwt]
  signingAlgorithms: [PS256]
  consentCarrier: intent-claim
  accessTokenSeconds: 300
# Internal consent state to the wire code of the documents, per consent family.
consentStatus:
  account-access:     {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT, revoked: CANC, expired: EXPD}
  payment:            {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT, consumed: COND}
  funds-confirmation: {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT, revoked: CANC, expired: EXPD}
  vrp:                {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT, revoked: CANC, expired: EXPD}
idempotencyHours: 24
```

<!-- BEGIN SOURCE.md -->
````markdown
# Source of profile rw-4.0.0

The six OpenAPI documents in this folder are the publisher's files of the Read/Write API 4.0.0 of the UK open-banking standard. They are copied byte for byte and are never edited. A newer release of the standard is a new profile folder.

- Publisher: Open Banking Limited, repository https://github.com/OpenBankingUK/read-write-api-specs
- Tag: `v4.0-Update-5`, the newest tag whose documents carry `info.version: 4.0.0`
- Commit: `5af5621e1b68dda5a0d821f2ffee9d282cfe550a`
- Folder in that repository: `dist/openapi/`
- Licence: the publisher's open licence, https://www.openbanking.org.uk/open-licence, named in the `info.license` field of every document

| File | Operations | SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|
| `account-info-openapi.yaml` | 29 | `7d9297935e11971173664ba793c14b6fd64e162a97d2de46967cd7f9cc0ca792` |
| `payment-initiation-openapi.yaml` | 41 | `1cb84c8b6972cb56f414448af94f5c7595d6ebe13a5c41deeba5904e04efb41e` |
| `vrp-openapi.yaml` | 9 | `f547f3cbc465970c65a744dce9c28fa5f0201fb5f07b8f35af79942e3c64fb20` |
| `confirmation-funds-openapi.yaml` | 4 | `2ab1717555e8e960980be39f07821e1c0d280662a0fbb84dd562168cb5604358` |
| `events-openapi.yaml` | 5 | `ba248d53243ddad30ee856e175f8df38669eac2b1015c7c10d88db56afbcb642` |
| `event-notifications-openapi.yaml` | 1 | `c60932f376472697c22af602d4deba5842921732aa547ef71d6c41a67389908a` |

`vrp-openapi.yaml` has Windows line endings in the publisher's repository. The file `.gitattributes` of this folder keeps every document free of line-ending conversion, so the checksums hold in every checkout.

## The answer for an operation that is not offered

`profile.yaml` sets `notImplementedStatus: 404`. The 25 international and file-payment operations lie under its `notOffered` prefixes and are answered with that status. The standard's Read/Write Data API Profile, version 4.0, section "HTTP Status Codes", part "400 (Bad Request) v/s 404 (Not Found)", says:

> If an ASPSP has not implemented an API endpoint, it **must** respond with a 404 (Not Found) for requests to that URL.

Address: https://openbankinguk.github.io/read-write-api-site3/v4.0/profiles/read-write-data-api-profile.html#_400-bad-request-v-s-404-not-found

The same part lists, among its examples, "A TPP attempts to retrieve a resource that is in the specification, but not implemented by the ASPSP" with the response 404 (Not Found).
````
<!-- END SOURCE.md -->

<!-- BEGIN README.md -->
````markdown
# Fingate: open banking

The open-banking product of Catalyst: an API that third parties call to read accounts and make payments for a bank's customers, under a public open-banking standard.

**Status: specification kit only.** No chart, no Blueprint and no running service ship yet. `docs/STATUS.md` is the inventory of what is built.

## Role in Catalyst

An Application Blueprint, `bp-fingate`, not published yet. It is installed in an Organization of its own, and that Organization's realm is the open-banking realm. It is written fresh in Go on platform services: Keycloak authorises customers, OpenBao signs and holds credentials, the PostgreSQL capability stores consents and payments. Core banking is reached only through a typed port that an adapter serves. The decision record is [ADR-0034](../../docs/adr/0034-later-products-ai-hub-and-open-banking.md).

## What is here

- `profiles/rw-4.0.0/`: the first standards profile. `profile.yaml` names the six OpenAPI documents of the Read/Write API 4.0.0, their operation counts, the path prefixes that are not offered, the security rules and the consent status codes. The documents are the publisher's files, unchanged; `SOURCE.md` says where they come from and gives their checksums.
- `internal/profile/`: the Go package that loads a profile, checks each document against its checksum and counts its operations.

## Configuration

The standard is data, not code. A profile is one folder under `profiles/`, and its folder name is its identifier. One profile will be active per Application; no Blueprint parameter selects it yet.

## Operations

Run the tests with `cd products/fingate && go test ./...`. A vendored document is never edited: a newer release of the standard is a new profile folder with its own `SOURCE.md`.
````

<!-- BEGIN profile.go -->
```go
// Package profile loads and checks a standards profile of the open-banking product.
//
// A profile is one folder under products/fingate/profiles/<id>/. It holds profile.yaml and
// the OpenAPI documents that profile.yaml names, vendored from the publisher unchanged.
// Load reads profile.yaml, rejects an unknown or a missing field, checks every value,
// checks each document against its SHA-256, counts the operations of each document and
// asserts that served plus called plus the operations under a notOffered prefix equals
// the operation count of the document.
package profile

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
)

// Schema is the only value of the field schema.
const Schema = "openova.io/fingate-profile/v1"

// FileName is the name of the profile file inside a profile folder.
const FileName = "profile.yaml"

// SignatureHeader is the request header of a detached signature.
const SignatureHeader = "x-jws-signature"

// Profile is the content of profile.yaml, with the operations read from its documents.
type Profile struct {
	Schema               string                       `yaml:"schema"`
	ID                   string                       `yaml:"id"`
	BasePath             string                       `yaml:"basePath"`
	NotImplementedStatus int                          `yaml:"notImplementedStatus"`
	Documents            []Document                   `yaml:"documents"`
	NotOffered           []string                     `yaml:"notOffered"`
	Security             Security                     `yaml:"security"`
	ConsentStatus        map[string]map[string]string `yaml:"consentStatus"`
	IdempotencyHours     int                          `yaml:"idempotencyHours"`
}

// Document is one OpenAPI document of a profile.
type Document struct {
	API        string `yaml:"api"`
	File       string `yaml:"file"`
	SHA256     string `yaml:"sha256"`
	Operations int    `yaml:"operations"`
	Served     int    `yaml:"served"`
	Called     int    `yaml:"called"`

	// Ops holds the operations of the document, sorted by path and method. Load fills it.
	Ops []Operation `yaml:"-"`
}

// Operation is one operation of a document.
type Operation struct {
	Method string // upper case, for example POST
	Path   string // the key of the document's paths object
	Signed bool   // the operation declares the header x-jws-signature as required
}

// Security holds the security rules of a profile.
type Security struct {
	FAPI               string   `yaml:"fapi"`
	ClientAuth         []string `yaml:"clientAuth"`
	SigningAlgorithms  []string `yaml:"signingAlgorithms"`
	ConsentCarrier     string   `yaml:"consentCarrier"`
	AccessTokenSeconds int      `yaml:"accessTokenSeconds"`
}

var (
	hexSHA256 = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{64}$`)
	wireCode  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Z]{4}$`)

	methods = map[string]bool{"get": true, "put": true, "post": true, "delete": true,
		"options": true, "head": true, "patch": true, "trace": true}
	clientAuths     = map[string]bool{"tls_client_auth": true, "private_key_jwt": true}
	algorithms      = map[string]bool{"PS256": true}
	consentCarriers = map[string]bool{"intent-claim": true, "authorization-details": true}
	families        = []string{"account-access", "funds-confirmation", "payment", "vrp"}
	states          = map[string]bool{"awaiting": true, "authorised": true, "rejected": true,
		"revoked": true, "expired": true, "consumed": true}
)

// LoadDir loads the profile in the folder dir. The folder name is the profile identifier.
func LoadDir(dir string) (*Profile, error) {
	return Load(os.DirFS(dir), filepath.Base(filepath.Clean(dir)))
}

// Load loads the profile whose folder is fsys and whose identifier is id.
func Load(fsys fs.FS, id string) (*Profile, error) {
	raw, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, FileName)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var node yaml.Node
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(raw, &node); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", FileName, err)
	}
	if err := required(&node); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", FileName, err)
	}
	var p Profile
	dec := yaml.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
	dec.KnownFields(true)
	if err := dec.Decode(&p); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", FileName, err)
	}
	if err := p.check(id); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", FileName, err)
	}
	for i := range p.Documents {
		if err := p.readDocument(fsys, &p.Documents[i]); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return &p, nil
}

// NotOfferedPath reports whether a document path lies under a notOffered prefix: it equals
// the prefix, or it starts with the prefix followed by "/".
func (p *Profile) NotOfferedPath(docPath string) bool {
	for _, prefix := range p.NotOffered {
		if docPath == prefix || strings.HasPrefix(docPath, prefix+"/") {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// SignedCount is the number of operations of the document that declare the signature header.
func (d *Document) SignedCount() int {
	n := 0
	for _, op := range d.Ops {
		if op.Signed {
			n++
		}
	}
	return n
}

// required reports the first required key that profile.yaml leaves out.
func required(doc *yaml.Node) error {
	if doc.Kind != yaml.DocumentNode || len(doc.Content) != 1 || doc.Content[0].Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
		return errors.New("the file is not one YAML mapping")
	}
	top := doc.Content[0]
	if err := keys(top, "", "schema", "id", "basePath", "notImplementedStatus", "documents",
		"notOffered", "security", "consentStatus", "idempotencyHours"); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if sec := value(top, "security"); sec != nil {
		if err := keys(sec, "security.", "fapi", "clientAuth", "signingAlgorithms",
			"consentCarrier", "accessTokenSeconds"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if docs := value(top, "documents"); docs != nil && docs.Kind == yaml.SequenceNode {
		for i, d := range docs.Content {
			if err := keys(d, fmt.Sprintf("documents[%d].", i), "api", "file", "sha256",
				"operations", "served"); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func keys(m *yaml.Node, where string, names ...string) error {
	if m.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s is not a mapping", strings.TrimSuffix(where, "."))
	}
	for _, n := range names {
		if value(m, n) == nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("field %s%s is missing", where, n)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func value(m *yaml.Node, key string) *yaml.Node {
	if m.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
		return nil
	}
	for i := 0; i+1 < len(m.Content); i += 2 {
		if m.Content[i].Value == key {
			return m.Content[i+1]
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// check applies every rule that needs no document. It returns all broken rules, one per line.
func (p *Profile) check(id string) error {
	var errs []error
	bad := func(format string, a ...any) { errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf(format, a...)) }
	if p.Schema != Schema {
		bad("schema is %q, want %q", p.Schema, Schema)
	}
	if p.ID == "" || p.ID != id {
		bad("id is %q, want the folder name %q", p.ID, id)
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(p.BasePath, "/") || strings.HasSuffix(p.BasePath, "/") {
		bad("basePath %q must start with / and must not end with /", p.BasePath)
	}
	if p.NotImplementedStatus < 400 || p.NotImplementedStatus > 499 {
		bad("notImplementedStatus %d is not a status from 400 to 499", p.NotImplementedStatus)
	}
	if len(p.Documents) == 0 {
		bad("documents is empty")
	}
	apis, files := map[string]bool{}, map[string]bool{}
	for i, d := range p.Documents {
		if d.API == "" || apis[d.API] {
			bad("documents[%d]: api %q is empty or not unique", i, d.API)
		}
		apis[d.API] = true
		if d.File == "" || files[d.File] || path.Base(d.File) != d.File || !fs.ValidPath(d.File) {
			bad("documents[%d]: file %q is empty, not unique, or not a plain file name", i, d.File)
		}
		files[d.File] = true
		if !hexSHA256.MatchString(d.SHA256) {
			bad("documents[%d]: sha256 %q is not 64 lower-case hex characters", i, d.SHA256)
		}
		if d.Operations < 1 || d.Served < 0 || d.Called < 0 || d.Served+d.Called > d.Operations {
			bad("documents[%d]: operations %d, served %d, called %d do not fit", i, d.Operations, d.Served, d.Called)
		}
	}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, prefix := range p.NotOffered {
		if !strings.HasPrefix(prefix, "/") || strings.HasSuffix(prefix, "/") || seen[prefix] {
			bad("notOffered %q must start with /, must not end with / and must be unique", prefix)
		}
		seen[prefix] = true
	}
	s := p.Security
	if s.FAPI != "fapi1-advanced-final" {
		bad("security.fapi %q is not fapi1-advanced-final", s.FAPI)
	}
	if !members(s.ClientAuth, clientAuths) {
		bad("security.clientAuth %q must be a non-empty list of different values, each tls_client_auth or private_key_jwt", s.ClientAuth)
	}
	if !members(s.SigningAlgorithms, algorithms) {
		bad("security.signingAlgorithms %q must be a non-empty list of different values, each PS256", s.SigningAlgorithms)
	}
	if !consentCarriers[s.ConsentCarrier] {
		bad("security.consentCarrier %q is not intent-claim or authorization-details", s.ConsentCarrier)
	}
	if s.AccessTokenSeconds < 60 || s.AccessTokenSeconds > 600 {
		bad("security.accessTokenSeconds %d is not from 60 to 600", s.AccessTokenSeconds)
	}
	got := make([]string, 0, len(p.ConsentStatus))
	for f := range p.ConsentStatus {
		got = append(got, f)
	}
	sort.Strings(got)
	if strings.Join(got, ",") != strings.Join(families, ",") {
		bad("consentStatus has the families %q, want exactly %q", got, families)
	}
	for _, f := range got {
		codes := p.ConsentStatus[f]
		for _, st := range []string{"awaiting", "authorised", "rejected"} {
			if _, ok := codes[st]; !ok {
				bad("consentStatus.%s has no code for the state %s", f, st)
			}
		}
		for st, code := range codes {
			if !states[st] {
				bad("consentStatus.%s: %q is not a consent state", f, st)
			}
			if st == "consumed" && f != "payment" {
				bad("consentStatus.%s: only the payment family has the state consumed", f)
			}
			if !wireCode.MatchString(code) {
				bad("consentStatus.%s.%s: %q is not four capital letters", f, st, code)
			}
		}
	}
	if p.IdempotencyHours < 1 {
		bad("idempotencyHours %d is below 1", p.IdempotencyHours)
	}
	return errors.Join(errs...)
}

func members(list []string, allowed map[string]bool) bool {
	if len(list) == 0 {
		return false
	}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, v := range list {
		if !allowed[v] || seen[v] {
			return false
		}
		seen[v] = true
	}
	return true
}

// openAPI is the part of an OpenAPI 3.0 document that Load reads.
type openAPI struct {
	OpenAPI    string                          `yaml:"openapi"`
	Paths      map[string]map[string]yaml.Node `yaml:"paths"`
	Components struct {
		Parameters map[string]parameter `yaml:"parameters"`
	} `yaml:"components"`
}

type parameter struct {
	Ref      string `yaml:"$ref"`
	In       string `yaml:"in"`
	Name     string `yaml:"name"`
	Required bool   `yaml:"required"`
}

type operation struct {
	Parameters []parameter `yaml:"parameters"`
}

// readDocument checks the SHA-256 of one document, reads its operations and checks the counts.
func (p *Profile) readDocument(fsys fs.FS, d *Document) error {
	where := "document " + d.File
	raw, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, d.File)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", where, err)
	}
	sum := sha256.Sum256(raw)
	if got := hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]); got != d.SHA256 {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: SHA-256 is %s, the profile says %s", where, got, d.SHA256)
	}
	var doc openAPI
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(raw, &doc); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", where, err)
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(doc.OpenAPI, "3.0.") {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: openapi is %q, want a version 3.0", where, doc.OpenAPI)
	}
	resolve := func(q parameter) (parameter, error) {
		if q.Ref == "" {
			return q, nil
		}
		name, ok := strings.CutPrefix(q.Ref, "#/components/parameters/")
		target, found := doc.Components.Parameters[name]
		if !ok || !found || target.Ref != "" {
			return q, fmt.Errorf("%s: parameter reference %q is not a parameter of the document", where, q.Ref)
		}
		return target, nil
	}
	d.Ops = nil
	for docPath, item := range doc.Paths {
		if _, ok := item["$ref"]; ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("%s: path %s is a reference, which this loader does not read", where, docPath)
		}
		var shared []parameter
		if n, ok := item["parameters"]; ok {
			if err := n.Decode(&shared); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("%s: path %s: %w", where, docPath, err)
			}
		}
		for key, n := range item {
			if !methods[key] {
				continue
			}
			var op operation
			if err := n.Decode(&op); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("%s: %s %s: %w", where, key, docPath, err)
			}
			signed := false
			for _, q := range append(append([]parameter{}, shared...), op.Parameters...) {
				r, err := resolve(q)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				if r.In == "header" && strings.EqualFold(r.Name, SignatureHeader) && r.Required {
					signed = true
				}
			}
			d.Ops = append(d.Ops, Operation{Method: strings.ToUpper(key), Path: docPath, Signed: signed})
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(d.Ops, func(i, j int) bool {
		if d.Ops[i].Path != d.Ops[j].Path {
			return d.Ops[i].Path < d.Ops[j].Path
		}
		return d.Ops[i].Method < d.Ops[j].Method
	})
	if len(d.Ops) != d.Operations {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: it has %d operations, the profile says %d", where, len(d.Ops), d.Operations)
	}
	notOffered := 0
	for _, op := range d.Ops {
		if p.NotOfferedPath(op.Path) {
			notOffered++
		}
	}
	if d.Served+d.Called+notOffered != d.Operations {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: served %d plus called %d plus not offered %d is not the %d operations",
			where, d.Served, d.Called, notOffered, d.Operations)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
package profile

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"

	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
)

const rw400Dir = "../../profiles/rw-4.0.0"

// want holds the facts of one document of profile rw-4.0.0, counted from the publisher's files.
type want struct {
	api, file, sha256                  string
	operations, served, called, signed int
}

var rw400 = []want{
	{"account-info", "account-info-openapi.yaml", "7d9297935e11971173664ba793c14b6fd64e162a97d2de46967cd7f9cc0ca792", 29, 29, 0, 0},
	{"payment-initiation", "payment-initiation-openapi.yaml", "1cb84c8b6972cb56f414448af94f5c7595d6ebe13a5c41deeba5904e04efb41e", 41, 16, 0, 15},
	{"vrp", "vrp-openapi.yaml", "f547f3cbc465970c65a744dce9c28fa5f0201fb5f07b8f35af79942e3c64fb20", 9, 9, 0, 5},
	{"confirmation-funds", "confirmation-funds-openapi.yaml", "2ab1717555e8e960980be39f07821e1c0d280662a0fbb84dd562168cb5604358", 4, 4, 0, 0},
	{"events", "events-openapi.yaml", "ba248d53243ddad30ee856e175f8df38669eac2b1015c7c10d88db56afbcb642", 5, 5, 0, 2},
	{"event-notifications", "event-notifications-openapi.yaml", "c60932f376472697c22af602d4deba5842921732aa547ef71d6c41a67389908a", 1, 0, 1, 0},
}

func loadRW400(t *testing.T) *Profile {
	t.Helper()
	p, err := LoadDir(rw400Dir)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("LoadDir(%s): %v", rw400Dir, err)
	}
	return p
}

// TestRW400Profile asserts each hash, each operation count, the totals and the signature-header counts.
func TestRW400Profile(t *testing.T) {
	p := loadRW400(t)
	if len(p.Documents) != len(rw400) {
		t.Fatalf("documents: got %d, want %d", len(p.Documents), len(rw400))
	}
	total, served, called, notOffered := 0, 0, 0, 0
	for i, w := range rw400 {
		d := p.Documents[i]
		if d.API != w.api || d.File != w.file || d.SHA256 != w.sha256 {
			t.Errorf("documents[%d]: got %s %s %s, want %s %s %s", i, d.API, d.File, d.SHA256, w.api, w.file, w.sha256)
		}
		if d.Operations != w.operations || len(d.Ops) != w.operations || d.Served != w.served || d.Called != w.called {
			t.Errorf("%s: operations %d (read %d), served %d, called %d; want %d, %d, %d",
				w.api, d.Operations, len(d.Ops), d.Served, d.Called, w.operations, w.served, w.called)
		}
		if got := d.SignedCount(); got != w.signed {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d operations declare %s, want %d", w.api, got, SignatureHeader, w.signed)
		}
		total += len(d.Ops)
		served += d.Served
		called += d.Called
		for _, op := range d.Ops {
			if p.NotOfferedPath(op.Path) {
				notOffered++
			}
		}
	}
	if total != 89 || served != 63 || called != 1 || notOffered != 25 {
		t.Errorf("totals: %d operations, %d served, %d called, %d not offered; want 89, 63, 1, 25", total, served, called, notOffered)
	}
	if p.ID != "rw-4.0.0" || p.BasePath != "/open-banking/v4.0" || p.NotImplementedStatus != 404 || p.IdempotencyHours != 24 {
		t.Errorf("got id %q, basePath %q, notImplementedStatus %d, idempotencyHours %d", p.ID, p.BasePath, p.NotImplementedStatus, p.IdempotencyHours)
	}
	wantNotOffered := []string{"/international-payment-consents", "/international-payments",
		"/international-scheduled-payment-consents", "/international-scheduled-payments",
		"/international-standing-order-consents", "/international-standing-orders",
		"/file-payment-consents", "/file-payments"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(p.NotOffered, wantNotOffered) {
		t.Errorf("notOffered: got %q", p.NotOffered)
	}
	wantSecurity := Security{FAPI: "fapi1-advanced-final", ClientAuth: []string{"tls_client_auth", "private_key_jwt"},
		SigningAlgorithms: []string{"PS256"}, ConsentCarrier: "intent-claim", AccessTokenSeconds: 300}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(p.Security, wantSecurity) {
		t.Errorf("security: got %+v", p.Security)
	}
}

// consentSchemas names, per consent family, the consent response schemas whose Data.Status
// enumeration holds the family's wire codes in the documents of rw-4.0.0.
var consentSchemas = map[string]struct {
	file    string
	schemas []string
}{
	"account-access":     {"account-info-openapi.yaml", []string{"OBReadConsentResponse1"}},
	"payment":            {"payment-initiation-openapi.yaml", []string{"OBWriteDomesticConsentResponse5", "OBWriteDomesticScheduledConsentResponse5", "OBWriteDomesticStandingOrderConsentResponse6"}},
	"funds-confirmation": {"confirmation-funds-openapi.yaml", []string{"OBFundsConfirmationConsentResponse1"}},
	"vrp":                {"vrp-openapi.yaml", []string{"OBDomesticVRPConsentResponse"}},
}

// TestRW400ConsentCodes asserts the consent status map and that every code is one the documents declare.
func TestRW400ConsentCodes(t *testing.T) {
	p := loadRW400(t)
	wantStatus := map[string]map[string]string{
		"account-access":     {"awaiting": "AWAU", "authorised": "AUTH", "rejected": "RJCT", "revoked": "CANC", "expired": "EXPD"},
		"payment":            {"awaiting": "AWAU", "authorised": "AUTH", "rejected": "RJCT", "consumed": "COND"},
		"funds-confirmation": {"awaiting": "AWAU", "authorised": "AUTH", "rejected": "RJCT", "revoked": "CANC", "expired": "EXPD"},
		"vrp":                {"awaiting": "AWAU", "authorised": "AUTH", "rejected": "RJCT", "revoked": "CANC", "expired": "EXPD"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(p.ConsentStatus, wantStatus) {
		t.Errorf("consentStatus: got %v", p.ConsentStatus)
	}
	fsys := osDir(t)
	for family, codes := range p.ConsentStatus {
		cs := consentSchemas[family]
		doc := readYAML(t, fsys, cs.file)
		for _, schema := range cs.schemas {
			enum := map[string]bool{}
			for _, v := range dig(t, doc, "components", "schemas", schema, "properties", "Data", "properties", "Status", "enum").([]any) {
				enum[v.(string)] = true
			}
			for state, code := range codes {
				if !enum[code] {
					t.Errorf("%s.%s: code %s is not in the Status enumeration of %s in %s", family, state, code, schema, cs.file)
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

// TestRW400Documents asserts that each document is OpenAPI 3.0.0 of version 4.0.0 and that the
// files on disk hash to the values the profile states.
func TestRW400Documents(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := osDir(t)
	for _, w := range rw400 {
		raw, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, w.file)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		sum := sha256.Sum256(raw)
		if hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]) != w.sha256 {
			t.Errorf("%s: the file does not hash to %s", w.file, w.sha256)
		}
		doc := readYAML(t, fsys, w.file)
		if doc["openapi"] != "3.0.0" || dig(t, doc, "info", "version") != "4.0.0" {
			t.Errorf("%s: openapi %v, info.version %v; want 3.0.0 and 4.0.0", w.file, doc["openapi"], dig(t, doc, "info", "version"))
		}
	}
}

// TestLoadRejects proves that each rule of the loader can fail.
func TestLoadRejects(t *testing.T) {
	// first is the documents line of the fixture. second returns it with one more document after it.
	const first = "  - {api: a, file: a.yaml, sha256: SUM, operations: 3, served: 1, called: 1}\n"
	second := func(api, file string) string {
		return first + "  - {api: " + api + ", file: " + file + ", sha256: " + strings.Repeat("0", 64) + ", operations: 1, served: 1}\n"
	}
	cases := []struct{ name, find, replace, err string }{
		{"wrong schema", "schema: openova.io/fingate-profile/v1", "schema: other/v1", "schema is"},
		{"id is not the folder", "id: p1", "id: p2", "want the folder name"},
		{"unknown field", "idempotencyHours: 24", "idempotencyHours: 24\nextra: 1", "field extra not found"},
		{"missing field", "idempotencyHours: 24", "", "field idempotencyHours is missing"},
		{"missing served", "served: 1, ", "", "field documents[0].served is missing"},
		{"bad basePath", "basePath: /ob", "basePath: ob", "basePath"},
		{"status out of range", "notImplementedStatus: 404", "notImplementedStatus: 200", "notImplementedStatus"},
		{"api twice", first, second("a", "b.yaml"), `documents[1]: api "a" is empty or not unique`},
		{"file twice", first, second("b", "a.yaml"), `documents[1]: file "a.yaml" is empty, not unique`},
		{"file with a folder", "file: a.yaml", "file: x/a.yaml", "not a plain file name"},
		{"sha256 not hex", "sha256: SUM", "sha256: xyz", "is not 64 lower-case hex"},
		{"wrong hash", "sha256: SUM", "sha256: " + strings.Repeat("0", 64), "SHA-256 is"},
		{"counts do not fit", "served: 1", "served: 3", "do not fit"},
		{"wrong operation count", "operations: 3", "operations: 2", "it has 3 operations, the profile says 2"},
		{"sum does not hold", "served: 1", "served: 0", "is not the 3 operations"},
		{"bad notOffered", "  - /b", "  - b", "notOffered"},
		{"bad fapi", "fapi: fapi1-advanced-final", "fapi: fapi2", "security.fapi"},
		{"bad client auth", "clientAuth: [tls_client_auth]", "clientAuth: [client_secret_basic]", "security.clientAuth"},
		{"bad algorithm", "signingAlgorithms: [PS256]", "signingAlgorithms: [RS256]", "security.signingAlgorithms"},
		{"bad carrier", "consentCarrier: intent-claim", "consentCarrier: other", "security.consentCarrier"},
		{"token lifetime", "accessTokenSeconds: 300", "accessTokenSeconds: 30", "security.accessTokenSeconds"},
		{"missing family", "  vrp: {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT}\n", "", "consentStatus has the families"},
		{"unknown state", "revoked: CANC", "cancelled: CANC", "is not a consent state"},
		{"consumed outside payment", "vrp: {awaiting: AWAU,", "vrp: {consumed: COND, awaiting: AWAU,", "only the payment family"},
		{"missing state", "funds-confirmation: {awaiting: AWAU, ", "funds-confirmation: {", "no code for the state awaiting"},
		{"bad code", "rejected: RJCT, revoked", "rejected: rjct, revoked", "is not four capital letters"},
		{"idempotency below 1", "idempotencyHours: 24", "idempotencyHours: 0", "idempotencyHours 0"},
	}
	if _, err := Load(fixture(t, "", ""), "p1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the valid fixture does not load: %v", err)
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := Load(fixture(t, c.find, c.replace), "p1")
			if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), c.err) {
				t.Errorf("got error %v, want one that contains %q", err, c.err)
			}
		})
	}
	t.Run("broken parameter reference", func(t *testing.T) {
		doc := strings.Replace(fixtureDoc, "#/components/parameters/sig", "#/components/parameters/none", 1)
		if _, err := Load(fixtureWith(t, "", "", doc), "p1"); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "is not a parameter of the document") {
			t.Errorf("got error %v", err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("document is not OpenAPI 3.0", func(t *testing.T) {
		doc := strings.Replace(fixtureDoc, "openapi: 3.0.0", "openapi: 3.1.0", 1)
		if _, err := Load(fixtureWith(t, "", "", doc), "p1"); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "want a version 3.0") {
			t.Errorf("got error %v", err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("signature header is counted", func(t *testing.T) {
		p, err := Load(fixture(t, "", ""), "p1")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if got := p.Documents[0].SignedCount(); got != 1 {
			t.Errorf("signed operations: got %d, want 1", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("an optional signature header is not counted", func(t *testing.T) {
		doc := strings.Replace(fixtureDoc, "required: true", "required: false", 1)
		p, err := Load(fixtureWith(t, "", "", doc), "p1")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if got := p.Documents[0].SignedCount(); got != 0 {
			t.Errorf("signed operations: got %d, want 0", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a prefix covers whole path segments", func(t *testing.T) {
		p, err := Load(fixture(t, "", ""), "p1")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		for docPath, want := range map[string]bool{"/b": true, "/b/{id}": true, "/bc": false, "/a/b": false} {
			if got := p.NotOfferedPath(docPath); got != want {
				t.Errorf("NotOfferedPath(%s): got %v, want %v", docPath, got, want)
			}
		}
	})
}

// fixtureDoc has three operations: GET /a, POST /a, which declares the signature header, and
// GET /b/{id}, which lies under the notOffered prefix /b. fixtureProfile counts one served, one
// called and one not offered.
const fixtureDoc = `openapi: 3.0.0
info: {version: 1.0.0}
paths:
  /a:
    get: {responses: {}}
    post:
      parameters:
        - $ref: '#/components/parameters/sig'
      responses: {}
  /b/{id}:
    get: {responses: {}}
components:
  parameters:
    sig: {in: header, name: x-jws-signature, required: true, schema: {type: string}}
`

const fixtureProfile = `schema: openova.io/fingate-profile/v1
id: p1
basePath: /ob
notImplementedStatus: 404
documents:
  - {api: a, file: a.yaml, sha256: SUM, operations: 3, served: 1, called: 1}
notOffered:
  - /b
security:
  fapi: fapi1-advanced-final
  clientAuth: [tls_client_auth]
  signingAlgorithms: [PS256]
  consentCarrier: intent-claim
  accessTokenSeconds: 300
consentStatus:
  account-access: {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT, revoked: CANC}
  payment: {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT, consumed: COND}
  funds-confirmation: {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT}
  vrp: {awaiting: AWAU, authorised: AUTH, rejected: RJCT}
idempotencyHours: 24
`

func fixture(t *testing.T, find, replace string) fs.FS {
	return fixtureWith(t, find, replace, fixtureDoc)
}

func fixtureWith(t *testing.T, find, replace, doc string) fs.FS {
	t.Helper()
	text := fixtureProfile
	if find != "" {
		if strings.Count(text, find) != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("fixture: %q does not occur exactly once", find)
		}
		text = strings.Replace(text, find, replace, 1)
	}
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(doc))
	text = strings.Replace(text, "SUM", hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]), 1)
	return fstest.MapFS{
		FileName: &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(text)},
		"a.yaml": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(doc)},
	}
}

func osDir(t *testing.T) fs.FS {
	t.Helper()
	return os.DirFS(rw400Dir)
}

func readYAML(t *testing.T, fsys fs.FS, name string) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, name)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var doc map[string]any
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(raw, &doc); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s: %v", name, err)
	}
	return doc
}

func dig(t *testing.T, node any, keys ...string) any {
	t.Helper()
	for _, k := range keys {
		m, ok := node.(map[string]any)
		if !ok {
			t.Fatalf("%v: not a mapping at %s", keys, k)
		}
		node, ok = m[k]
		if !ok {
			t.Fatalf("%v: no key %s", keys, k)
		}
	}
	return node
}
```

## 21. Return format
The marker file `docs/ledger/plan/done/PRD-101.yaml` in the same pull request, and a pull-request body in this form:

```
What changed:
- products/fingate/profiles/rw-4.0.0/*-openapi.yaml: the six documents of the Read/Write API 4.0.0, byte for byte from the publisher (tag v4.0-Update-5)
- products/fingate/profiles/rw-4.0.0/profile.yaml: the profile rw-4.0.0 with the checksums and counts of the six documents
- products/fingate/profiles/rw-4.0.0/SOURCE.md: where the documents come from, and the clause that sets 404 for an operation not offered
- products/fingate/profiles/rw-4.0.0/.gitattributes: no line-ending conversion for the documents
- products/fingate/internal/profile/profile.go: the loader that checks a profile and its documents
- products/fingate/internal/profile/profile_test.go: tests of the checksums, counts, consent codes and every loader rule
- products/fingate/go.mod, products/fingate/go.sum: the Go module of the product, with gopkg.in/yaml.v3
- products/fingate/README.md: rewritten in place as a short page
- .github/workflows/test-ungated-trees.yaml: products/fingate added to the path filters and to the go-modules matrix

Acceptance: commands A1 to A4 of the ticket, run by the check `work-package / acceptance`.

Refs #<issue>

Work-Package: PRD-101
```

## If this ticket is wrong

A ticket is wrong when a stop condition of field 17 is met. Do not repair the ticket. Do not try another way. Nothing is merged, so nothing is reverted.

- Leave the branch `wp/PRD-101` as it is. Do not delete it and do not push to it again. The architect deletes it when the ticket is amended.
- Run `git stash --include-untracked` if you have changes, then `git fetch origin` and `git switch -c wrong/PRD-101-rev1 origin/main`.
- Run `mkdir -p docs/ledger/plan/wrong`. Create the file `docs/ledger/plan/wrong/PRD-101-rev1.yaml` with these lines, each starting at the first column and each value filled with a fact:
  ```
  schema: openova.io/plan-wrong/v1
  id: PRD-101
  ticket_rev: 1
  base_commit: <output of: git rev-parse origin/main>
  stage: <one of: precondition, preflight, step, acceptance, scope, budget, check>
  ref: <the identifier or the step name in this ticket: P3, step 4, A2, changed paths, fence run>
  command: |
    <the command you ran, as you typed it, indented by four spaces>
  expected: |
    <what the ticket says, indented by four spaces>
  actual: |
    <what you got, each line indented by four spaces; at most the last 40 lines>
  reported_at: <output of: date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ>
  ```
- Run `git add -- docs/ledger/plan/wrong/PRD-101-rev1.yaml`. Commit with the same identity and the message `wrong(PRD-101): <stage> <ref>`. Run `git push -u origin wrong/PRD-101-rev1`.
- Open the pull request: `gh pr create --base main --head wrong/PRD-101-rev1 --title 'wrong(PRD-101)' --body 'Work-Package: PRD-101'`. The command prints its address; the number at the end is `<report>`. Do not merge it: the architect does.
- If you have done the step "tracking issue", comment on the issue: `gh api -X POST repos/openova-io/openova/issues/<issue>/comments -f body='Stopped: wrong report in pull request #<report>.' --jq .html_url`. Leave the issue open.
